## Newly authored bindings and Storage rows

Each family has its own closed payload selector file, exact original source-schema selections and field joins, native producer/transition/ordering text, stable original-operation event/idempotency identity, historical-read route, custody/backup/restore, compatibility and withdrawal protocol. The 19 per-family reader names and precise source records are enumerated in `Plans/event_authority_w3g/contracts.json`; each is independently bound in its owner/SP unit. Application keys adopt CV-366 exactly; numeric/timestamp/wire/physical encodings stay with their existing owners and the reviewed phase-2 shared helper.

The Storage value tables in SP-1500..SP-1518 give actual existing value IDs, exact key shapes, schema refs and retention policies. These events add no persisted filtered projection, private checkpoint or second original-operation store. Every exact family reader adopts the existing SP-278 generic projector, reader and `event_record_index_checkpoint.v1:{storage_instance_id}` with full global-range proof. Thus no new Storage value family row is needed and `Plans/storage_value_registry.json` is unchanged. Native source, result and referenced artifact availability remains an independent original-owner prerequisite, never supplied by an all-true fixture.

## Criterion-12 oracle plan and check-11 boundary

`Plans/event_authority_w3g/oracle_plan.json` contains 1629 semantic/shared guard rows and 2309 explicit schema assertion sites. Each family has its own positives, negative mutation/refusal/effect expectation and isolation instructions. Every negative must originate from that branch's valid original and carry one defect; deterministic copies/commitments are recomputed while independent originals remain valid. A shared keyword/refusal never merges predicates. Each real same-field/same-predicate duplicate site is cut individually and then together using the shared tool. Unresolved implication/overlap is a phase-2 finding, never a manufactured proof.

Phase 2 starts after the lead rebases onto the reviewed NUMRULE and GUARDTOOL foundation. It writes executable fixtures with the shared exact number/date-time helper and proves the complete assertion inventory with `scripts/pm_guard_isolation.py`; then Sol and Astra review. These plans are not executable oracles and demonstrate no guard-removal PASS.

| Standing defect class | Exact plan coverage / current evidence |
|---|---|
| Substituted identities/digests or contradictory snapshots | Per-family source issuance, full source/hash/identity/field-join guards; executable mutations deferred. |
| Storage-assigned fields trusted from payload | storage_metadata guard; producer comparison explicitly excludes Storage metadata. |
| Retained history rejected by current state, or invalid/foreign history accepted | Original authenticated edition positives after live-state change; separate current visibility/withdrawal negatives. |
| One field read two ways / number variants / strict history | Exact type-before-value and shared NUMRULE qualification; no local cap/encoder/parser. Current 1.0/-0/1e0/01 and authenticated old-recipe cases are planned at their owning input boundaries. |
| Unresolved unvisited refs | All 19 new schemas and reachable refs checked eagerly; exact schema-resource URI map committed. |
| Inline/operation parts above 16 KiB | SP-337 for full payload and independent per-part SP-340 checks, with large-value positives and negatives. |
| Custody skipped on retry/replay/recovery / wrong family | Whole original source/first receipt/group and payload-dispatch guards on every path. |
| Masked negatives / overstated grades | Predicate-only cuts; dependency repair required; criterion 12 stays PARTIAL until actual shared-tool proof. |
| Wrong primitive/container types | Per-schema-site inventory plus original source exact types and wrong-type cases. |
| format silently skipped | Shared enforced owner timestamp profiles; no new payload timestamp or local timestamp parser. |

No lane-local numeric/timestamp or guard-isolation machinery was authored. The only ad-hoc verification code is a structural schema/reference checker outside the repository, not an event oracle.

## Owner investigation: goal.receipt.recorded

The exact dotted name occurs once in current non-generated canon: `Plans/.implementation_readiness/non_executable_closure_evidence.json:264`, with payload contract `Plans/plans_to_code_handoff.schema.json#/$defs/goal_completion_receipt`, a Goal Runtime certifier producer and child-receipt/source-control negative. The current Goal owner separately lists the registered underscore name `goal.receipt_recorded` and D-R09, whose original interpretation uses receipt-kind/certifier/child/worknode fields and older lifecycle states. Current Goal V2 is the text-only four-state model and retires Goal-owned child/role structure. Neither owner explicitly proves that the dotted marker is only a result label, an alias, or a second persisted fact. A schema/ref match is not that proof.

**Finding:** unresolved owner evidence; no new contract, registration, alias or exclusion is authored. Preserve all existing historical-only/current-writer constraints and let DG/DG-OWN continue their own units.

**Exact question:** Does goal.receipt.recorded denote only the saved goal_completion_receipt proof/notification, an alias of retained goal.receipt_recorded, or a separate saved-history family? Which current owner contract supplies that meaning while preserving the historical-only constraints and the retirement of Goal certifier/child-Goal structure?

## Phase-1 review round 1: findings and responses

**Reviewed head:** `fbd1b87ef950472b950e4922dfee5fbf4a641e6a`. **Fix owner/plan commit:** `e3e1d7b2b38274d7d3a4e4f8290b895f0bb4cd47`. The independent phase-1 review found B1 on all nineteen rows and B2 on the two Goal rows. Both findings are accepted and repaired in this round; no finding is disputed. Independent re-review remains required. These are planning-document and test-oracle plan changes, not executable oracle code.

### B1 — all nineteen families

**Where:** `Plans/event_authority_w3g/oracle_plan.json:1066` and every exact family's `guards`, `compound_guard_inventory` and case-execution rule. **Cause:** the original currentness negative did not test lease or mutation permission, and other compound predicates had unenumerated clauses. **Fix:** every family now has independent `write_lease`, `write_lease_scope`, `write_permission`, currentness, owner authority/epoch, complete CAS, original intent, captured Stop epoch and final fence cases. Lease-denial and write-permission-denial cases preserve valid currentness, sources, read permission, other writer authority and commitments. Effective Stop is action-specific: Pause/Cancel retain their owner's permitted control behavior; continuation/recovery cannot erase a stop. Goal cancellation pending and receipt fences are separate from Stop and ordinary pending.

Every expanded clause carries its own positive, one-defect mutation, expected refusal, `expected_new_effects=0`, branch restriction and predicate-only isolation instruction. Compound source/receipt issuance, scope and subject joins, EventRecord/receipt/group identity, metadata, payload forms, coverage/CURRENT authenticity, checkpoint identity/snapshot, original admission/edition, read permission/deletion/restrictions, passive effects, restore dependencies and payload dispatch are split. Domain clauses include frozen Build inputs and native participants, cancellation settlement, completion requirements, immutable revision lineage, destination Wizard intake, research meter/averaging/credit/input/delivery rules, Goal lifecycle/body/history joins and notebook required-set/barrier/failure/admission rules. Optional participants are tested only on their actual owner branch; no missing Project/run/Crew/Goal is fabricated.

Current write/retry checks apply at initial admission and after returning helpers before the actual guarded boundary. An already committed native result remains pending/resolvable through original custody without repetition. Retained history keeps its authenticated original admission/edition despite later lease, write-permission, owner/CAS/Stop/cancellation changes; current disclosure still applies. The full lane now has **1629** planned semantic/shared guards and **2309** payload assertion sites. No phase-2 implementation or guard-removal PASS is claimed.

### B2 — goal.paused and goal.resumed

**Where:** `Plans/event_authority_w3g/contracts.json:518`, `Plans/Goal_Runtime_System.md:335` (GRS-1512), and the corresponding GRS-1513 paragraph. **Cause:** the Project source manifest selected the historical closed V1 control while the Storage rows and current owner selected V2. **Fix:** both current Project selections and complete before/after body/control/receipt joins now use `Plans/goal_cancel_command_custody.schema.json#/$defs/StorageBodyControlV2`. Real-shape positives name the V2 wrapper `pm.storage_value.goal_body_control.v2`, version `2.0.0`, record `pm.goal.body_control.v2`, required fields and independent null cancellation fields. Separate negatives cover current V1 dispatch, nonnull `cancellation_pending`, nonnull `cancellation_receipt_ref`, foreign ordinary pending, control/receipt joins and historical installation provenance. The original V1 decoder is confined to authentic retained original installations/admissions; no default-null upgrade or old-event relabel is allowed. Retained V2 facts survive later live cancellation/Stop.

**Main authority, not an integration-only copy:** at fetched `origin/main` `73f2231a9ec1c93190adafcdde51030f028c8c2d`, `Plans/Goal_Runtime_System.md:6191-6193` (GRS-064) selects BodyControlV2 with independent cancellation pending for every current mutation/read/backup route. `Plans/storage_value_registry.json:63613-63640` selects `goal_body_control` schema `2.0.0` and explicitly includes pause/resume in V2 adoption. The row also confines V1 to its original authenticated installation. The schema-only positive passes the real V2 selector and is rejected by V1. **DG integration dependency: none for this V2 selection.** No DG or DG-OWN unit, shared schema or registry row was changed or copied. Application remains its separately selected GRS-086/CV-366 graph with no Project cast; missing genuine app custody remains unavailable.

The seed map is explicitly not a claimed transitive closure. Resolver implementation remains phase 2.

## Wave 3 phase 2: findings and responses

**Brief:** `lanes/W3-P2.md` (stream E, 2026-11-13). **Start:** `feb55826eb7c93ac450a8965d193a949b2804598`. **Merges (exact gated commits, `--no-ff`, no rebase):** guard tool `004bdedd632d9463f3535190a6ed5772338cdce1` → `0a1d8c2b4a` (adds only its 11 files); NUMRULE head `58c2816ff33365384d4039553f5efdb5370930e6` → `9a2e14b651` (163 conflicts, all in `Plans/_shards/**` and `Plans/.plan_index/**`, resolved by regenerating; the only W3G shard change is two line-offset headers). `git diff --quiet 004bdedd63 HEAD -- <the tool's files>` and `git diff --quiet 58c2816ff3 HEAD -- scripts/pm_event_numbers* scripts/pm_event_datetime* Plans/event_number_recipe_corpus.json Plans/event_datetime_inventory.json` both exit 0: neither foundation's files are edited. **Head:** checker and Plans head `d2c76a76c4` (all checks below ran at it; the report commit follows).

**Stage:** synthetic reference oracles over the prepared contracts. Native owner facts are synthetic (`scripts/pm_w3g_spec.py`); native execution stays NOT_RUN. No admission, registry append, Decision Log entry, Spec Lock/evidence edit or main push. No new IDs were minted.

### What phase 2 built

| Path | Role |
|---|---|
| `scripts/pm_w3g_events.py` | The complete checker. `build_world` derives one coherent original (native source and owner issuance, payload, EventRecord, SP-337 pack, Storage-issued metadata, first receipt and group, sealed write receipt, request, writer, reader, checkpoint and restore state); `observe` derives one observation per row; `check` refuses only through the generated tables, in the boundary's table order (`order`). Boundaries: `append` (current write), `retry` (current retry), `history` (retained original), `restore`. |
| `scripts/pm_w3g_table_*.py` | 61 Method B guard tables, 1,538 rows, generated by `scripts/pm-w3g-table-generate.py` (`--check` reports 0 stale). Each predicate is `value[field] is not True`. |
| `scripts/pm_w3g_spec.py` | Per-family synthetic native facts, payload field → fact maps, and the family owner predicates (182 rows; the 9 rows the review asked for are marked B1-R1/R2/R3). |
| `scripts/pm_w3g_cases.py` | 582 positives and 8,177 negatives: one single-defect negative per (family, row, scope) and extra boundary cases. A change to the stored event is followed by `refinish`, so every dependent copy, key, hash, pack, receipt commitment and sealed receipt follows it. |
| `scripts/pm-w3g-event-contracts.py` | Runner: every case must refuse first at exactly its row (an acceptance, another row or an exception fails); a refusal must leave the input unchanged; plus table currency, schema closure, field uniqueness, timestamp inventory, witness coverage, schema-site map, plan-guard map, retention cells, resolver checks and the number corpus. |
| `scripts/pm_w3g_numbers.py` | The shared NUMRULE corpus through the complete routes at every integer slot. |
| `scripts/pm_w3g_guard_suites.py`, `scripts/pm-w3g-guard-tables.py` | Adapter and driver for the gated tool, the landed DCN pattern: one tool run per table, controls and own-row negatives collected from the complete checker's observations; credit copied from the tool's report. |

**Owner-pin drift found (integration item, not a W3G file):** `Plans/assistant_plan_cancel_schema_resources.json` pins `https://puppetmaster.local/schemas/full-thread-runtime-contracts.v1.json` to `Plans/full_thread_runtime_contracts.schema.json` at SHA-256 `5fa07b1e…`, but that path now holds `926b1216…`; the pinned bytes survive only in the frozen copies under `Plans/workflow_standard_source_contracts/**`. **Unproved rows with proofs (each sits alone in its own table):**

- `en_scope_kind` / `EJ00_scope_kind`: Always refused first by EN01 or EJ01. EN01 validates the logical event against EventRecord 2.0.0, whose root allOf (Plans/event_record.schema.json:144, if/then at Plans/event_record.schema.json:156 and the second then) makes scope_kind=application require project_id=null and scope_kind=project require a nonempty project_id; EJ01 (scripts/pm_w3g_events.py:1276) requires event project_id == the authentic source project_id; the source scope is coherent (scripts/pm_w3g_events.py:353: application has project_id None, project has PROJECT). With EN01 and EJ01 holding, a null project_id forces both scopes to application and a nonempty one forces both to project, so EJ00 cannot be the first refusal.
- `en_original_verified` / `RR07_original_verified`: Always refused first by ID01, RR03, ST05, NM04 or RR05. The frozen verifier recomputes exactly: (1) the w3g@1 event_id (scripts/pm_w3g_events.py:570), the predicate of ID01 (scripts/pm_w3g_events.py:1310); (2) sha256(source bytes) = binding.source_commitment (scripts/pm_w3g_events.py:572), which RR03 already requires (scripts/pm_w3g_events.py:1348) by comparing the sealed binding with binding_of(w), whose source_commitment is that hash; (3) the receipt commitment (scripts/pm_w3g_events.py:575), the predicate of ST05 (scripts/pm_w3g_events.py:1325); (4) for the current edition producer_semantic_bytes (scripts/pm_w3g_events.py:578), the predicate of NM04 (scripts/pm_w3g_events.py:1247), which runs on history exactly when the sealed recipe is the current edition (scripts/pm_w3g_events.py:830); (5) the installed tuple of that family/scope/edition (scripts/pm_w3g_events.py:579), the predicate of RR05 (scripts/pm_w3g_events.py:1352). Every predicate RR07 evaluates is an earlier row, so RR07 is never the first refusal.
- `en_goal_paused_pause_state` / `FD01_pause_state`: Always refused first by the py enum row of before_state or its join row FJ<n>_before_state. The payload field before_state is built from the native fact before.state (scripts/pm_w3g_spec.py:295); the join row (scripts/pm_w3g_events.py:1339) requires the stored payload to equal that fact; the closed selector pins before_state by enum (Plans/event_authority_w3g/payloads/goal_paused.schema.json:140 in ProjectPayloadV1, the same in ApplicationPayloadV1). A fact outside the enum either reaches the payload (the py enum row refuses) or not (the join row refuses), so this row is never the first refusal.
- `en_goal_paused_pause_state_after` / `FD02_pause_state_after`: Always refused first by the py const row of after_state or its join row FJ<n>_after_state. The payload field after_state is built from the native fact after.state (scripts/pm_w3g_spec.py:295); the join row (scripts/pm_w3g_events.py:1339) requires the stored payload to equal that fact; the closed selector pins after_state by const (Plans/event_authority_w3g/payloads/goal_paused.schema.json:146 in ProjectPayloadV1, the same in ApplicationPayloadV1). A fact outside the const either reaches the payload (the py const row refuses) or not (the join row refuses), so this row is never the first refusal.
- `en_goal_resumed_resume_state_before` / `FD05_resume_state_before`: Always refused first by the py enum row of before_state or its join row FJ<n>_before_state. The payload field before_state is built from the native fact before.state (scripts/pm_w3g_spec.py:295); the join row (scripts/pm_w3g_events.py:1339) requires the stored payload to equal that fact; the closed selector pins before_state by enum (Plans/event_authority_w3g/payloads/goal_resumed.schema.json:142 in ProjectPayloadV1, the same in ApplicationPayloadV1). A fact outside the enum either reaches the payload (the py enum row refuses) or not (the join row refuses), so this row is never the first refusal.
- `en_goal_resumed_resume_state_after` / `FD06_resume_state_after`: Always refused first by the py const row of after_state or its join row FJ<n>_after_state. The payload field after_state is built from the native fact after.state (scripts/pm_w3g_spec.py:295); the join row (scripts/pm_w3g_events.py:1339) requires the stored payload to equal that fact; the closed selector pins after_state by const (Plans/event_authority_w3g/payloads/goal_resumed.schema.json:148 in ProjectPayloadV1, the same in ApplicationPayloadV1). ### Numbers

- **Shared calls at every integer slot:** `current_integer` (payload integer slots with schema minimum/maximum, const slots as min = max; `producer_sequence_id` minimum 0), `storage_uint64` (EventRecord `sequence_id`, first-receipt `sequence_id`, read-token `frontier_revision`), `producer_semantic_bytes`/`producer_semantic_digest` (CV-317 projection; retry conflict compares the digest), `qualified_jcs` (the notebook required-set digest), `storage_value_bytes`/`decode_storage_value` (`EVENT_VALUE`: stored value, pack bytes, physical encodability), `full_value_commitment` (CV-339 receipt commitment), `verify_payload_bytes` (SP-337 arithmetic cross-check), `current_append_policy` (current writers only), `RetainedDispatcher` with `RecipeTuple`/`OriginalBinding`/`OriginalCustody`/`FrozenRecipe` (retained reads). No lane-local number cap, parser or serializer.
- **Shared corpus through the complete checker** (`scripts/pm_w3g_numbers.py`): 82 spellings (19 refused at `loads_current_json` ingress), 11391 routes (3816 accepted, 7575 refused) at every payload integer slot, `producer_sequence_id` (append and retry), Storage `sequence_id` (append and retained read), the receipt alone and the read-token frontier: **pass**, 0 mismatches, 0 crashes. Expected outcomes are composed from the shared helpers only.
- **`python3 scripts/pm_event_numbers.py --self-test --scope numbers`:** exit 0, `"status": "pass"`.
- **4,400-digit integer:** no crash anywhere. On a current write it is refused at `PF08_physical_value` (SP-1100 EVENT_VALUE cannot encode it); in a request it is refused at `SC02_exact_types`; through every payload slot and the producer route it reaches a refusal row (corpus spelling `9`×4400, 0 crashes). DX/DS15/DF/DM's crash shape does not occur.
- **Fixture expectations the shared rule changes:** W3G had no executable fixtures before phase 2 (phase 1 was a plan), so no earlier expectation changes. The plan's number cases now resolve as: `1.0` and `-0.0` at an integer slot refuse at `NM02_producer_sequence` / `NM03_payload_integers` (`current_integer` integer_type, though jsonschema `integer` accepts them); `true` refuses at `EN01_envelope_schema` (a bool is not a JSON integer); `2^53+1` refuses at `NM04_producer_route` (JCS binary64); `2^64` refuses at `PF08_physical_value`; raw `01`, `1e0`, `-0` spellings refuse at `loads_current_json` ingress (corpus).
- **Item 10a:** the legacy recipe positive and its current-write negative (Retained reads) show the stricter rule applies to new writes only and a forged selection is refused.

### Timestamps

| Field | Shared check | Profile | Row |
|---|---|---|---|
| `AppendReceipt.acknowledged_at_utc` | `pm_event_datetime.date_time` | `storage_utc_z` (SP-347 / CV-253 Storage strict profile) | `TM04_ack_clock` |
| `EventRecord.observed_at_utc` | `pm_event_datetime.date_time` | `storage_utc_z` (CV-253 Storage strict profile) | `TM02_observed_clock` |
| `EventRecord.occurred_at_utc` | `pm_event_datetime.date_time` | `event_record` (CV-1101 broad EventRecord profile) | `TM01_occurred_clock` |
| `EventRecord.persisted_at_utc` | `pm_event_datetime.date_time` | `storage_utc_z` (CV-253 Storage strict profile) | `TM03_persisted_clock` |

The 19 payload selectors declare no timestamp (`timestamp_inventory` asserts no `format` and no clock field in any selector), so these four are every clock a W3G stored value carries. Witnesses: missing zone, `+00:00` and lowercase `z` on Storage fields, `24:00:00`, a trailing LF; a legitimate leap second is accepted on `occurred_at_utc` (broad profile).
Date-time inventory gate (`python3 scripts/pm_event_datetime_inventory.py`): exit 1; status fail, 97 failure rows, all 'mentioning container' rows (W3G CV-15xx prose and its Contracts_V0 shards, the regenerated plan index, the plan-migration snapshot span map, the copied ignored currentness edition, oracle_plan.json) plus 2 stale scan exclusions on regenerated plan-index files; no declared timestamp field is among them (rows by kind and path: drafts/W3G/phase2/datetime-gate-summary.json). As SWEEP-P2B addendum 2 directs, the lane uses the shared `date_time` predicate with the explicit profile per field and does not call `datetime_validator` or the inventory gate in a lane tree whose Plans edits drift the pinned hashes; the gate and `datetime_validator` run at batch-3 integration. Combined self-test `python3 scripts/pm_event_numbers.py --self-test`: exit 1 (refused before its cases by the same inventory check (owner_integration_checks.datetime_inventory_complete_and_classified=false); number scope passes separately). The timestamp review is no longer interim for these four fields.

### Retained reads

A retained read selects its recipe only from the receipt Storage sealed for that exact original at write time (`seal_write_receipt`, writer side only); `retained_read` composes the shared `RetainedDispatcher` with `W3GOriginalOwner`, which answers only from the one sealed receipt of the exact locator. New-write selection stays separate: the writer seals the recipe it used (`RECIPES[(family, scope, edition)]`); a reader never consults the current recipe or a default. Rows `rr` (`RR01..RR06`) and the entailed `RR07` (own table, written proof):

| Case | Row | Result |
|---|---|---|
| missing receipt | `RR01_receipt_present` | `unavailable`, bytes unchanged, no receipt created |
| ambiguous receipt (two for one locator) | `RR02_receipt_unique` | `unsupported`, bytes unchanged |
| receipt for another original (binding names another operation) | `RR03_receipt_binding` | `unavailable` |
| unknown recipe | `RR04_recipe_known` | `unsupported` |
| another family/scope edition | `RR05_recipe_edition` | `unavailable` |
| no original admission at write time | `RR06_historical_recipe_admission` | `unavailable` |
| forged legacy selection (sealed binding with a forged source commitment) | `RR03_receipt_binding` | `unavailable` |

Positives: a retained original written under the legacy producer recipe with `producer_sequence_id = 2^53+1` is read by its sealed legacy edition (item 10a), while the same value on a current write refuses at `NM04_producer_route`; retained history stays readable after writer lease loss, write-permission revocation, CAS/owner/Stop change and live admission withdrawal; projector-replay-only compatibility history is read on the passive path and refused on a current write (`EN04`); an empty matching set over a proven captured range is `available` with no event. The runner asserts, for every history case, that no receipt is created and no custody byte changes.
